分銷網絡擴張                     Distribution Network Expansion
於本年度，我們致力擴展在中國內地的分銷        During the year, we were committed to expand our distribution
版圖。分銷商網絡由三個增至七個合作夥         footprint in China. Our distributor network increased from three to
伴，加強市場覆蓋及滲透能力。值得注意的        seven partners, strengthening our market coverage and penetration
是，Innococo與中糧、屈臣氏等訂立策略     capabilities. Notably, Innococo entered into a strategic cooperation
性合作協議，使產品更廣泛進入主要零售、        agreement with COFCO, Watsons, and others, enabling broader
便利店及專營渠道。我們認為，中糧及屈臣        access to key retail, convenience store, and specialty channels. We
氏在全國的廣泛銷售網絡將有助補充我們現        believe COFCO and Watsons’ extensive nationwide sales network
有的分銷商及加強Innococo的銷售。重要的    will help complement our existing distributors and strengthen

     2025年下半年全球經濟環境持續逐步          The global economic landscape in the second half of 2025
     穩定，主要經濟體通脹進一步緩和，            continued to demonstrate gradual stabilisation, with inflation
     各國中央銀行亦開始放寬貨幣政策。            moderating further in major economies and central banks
     消費者信心穩步改善，但消費模式仍            beginning to ease monetary policies. Consumer confidence
     保持審慎。在此環境下，消費者繼續            showed steady improvement, although cautious spending
     優先考慮物有所值的產品，同時對產            patterns persisted. Amid this environment, consumers have
     品質量及品牌真確性保持謹慎。              continued to prioritise value-driven purchases while remaining
                                 discerning about product quality and brand authenticity.

     健康意識仍是消費行為的主要趨勢。            Health consciousness remains a defining trend in consumer
     受COVID-19疫情推動，對健康的持續        behavior. The sustained focus on wellness, accelerated by
     關注繼續帶動對天然、清潔標籤飲品            the COVID-19 pandemic, continues to drive demand for
     的需求。隨著消費者日漸尋求符合其            natural, clean-label beverages. Non-alcoholic and functional
     健康目標的產品，無酒精及功能飲品            drinks have maintained strong momentum as consumers
     維持強勁增長動力。全球消費模式持            increasingly seek products that align with their health goals.
     續轉向無酒精或低酒精替代品及即飲            The shift toward no- or low-alcohol alternatives and ready-to-
     健康飲品。                       drink wellness beverages continues to reshape consumption
                                 patterns globally.

     在競爭激烈的飲品行業中，品牌傳承            Brand heritage and authenticity have emerged as key
     及真確性已成為主要差異因素。消費            differentiators in the competitive beverage landscape.

I.   報告期內溢利                         I.   Profit for the Reporting Period

          的董事或僱員）
                ；或                             companies of the Company); or

     2025年股份激勵計劃的目的包括（其                   The purpose of the 2025 Share Incentive Scheme includes,
     中包括）：(a)表彰及獎勵合資格參與者                  among other things, (a) recognising and rewarding eligible

       之責任而引致財務損失的風險。            contract, leading to a financial loss. The Group’s
       本集團面對的信貸風險主要源自            exposure to credit risk arises primarily from trade and
       貿易及其他應收款項以及應收貸            other receivables, and loan receivables. For other
       款。就其他金融資產（包括現金            financial assets (including cash and cash equivalents), the
       及現金等價物）而言，本集團透            Group minimises credit risk by dealing exclusively with
       過僅與高信貸評級的對手方進行            high credit rating counterparties.
       交易以將信貸風險減至最低。

       流動資金風險為本集團因資金短            Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter

       本集團面對的流動資金風險主要            shortage of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity

       錯配。本集團的流動資金風險管            of financial assets and liabilities. The Group’s liquidity
       理政策是以集團為基礎管理流動            risk management policy is to manage liquidity risk on a
       資金風險，以維持充足的流動金            group basis, to maintain sufficient liquid financial assets

       財務現金流需求提供資金。本集            requirements. The Group finances its working capital

股息政策                       DIVIDEND POLICY
本公司已採納有關支付股息的政策。在考慮        The Company has adopted a policy on the payment of dividends.
支付股息時，須平衡(i)保留充足儲備以維持      In considering the payment of dividends, there shall be a balance
營運資金需求及未來增長；及(ii)讓股東分享     between (i) retaining adequate reserves for maintaining working
本公司溢利。                     capital requirements and for future growth; and (ii) allowing
                           shareholders to participate in the Company’s profits.

董事會在考慮建議或支付股息及釐定股息金        The Board will take into account, among other things, the following
額時，將考慮以下因素（其中包括）：          factors when considering the recommendation or payment of
                           dividends and in determining the dividend amount:

(a)   本集團的實際及預期財務表現；       (a)   the actual and expected financial performance of the Group;

(b)   本集團的預期營運資金需求、資本開     (b)   the Group’s expected working capital requirements, capital
      支要求及未來擴張計劃；                expenditure requirements and future expansion plans;

(c)   本公司及本集團各成員公司的保留盈     (c)   the amount of retained earnings and distributable reserves of
      利及可分派儲備金額，以及本公司自           the Company and each of the members of the Group, and the
      附屬公司收取的分派；                 distributions received by the Company from its subsidiaries;

(d)   本集團的現金流量及流動資金狀況；     (d)   the Group’s cash flow and liquidity position;

(e)   現行經濟及市場狀況、本集團業務的     (e)   prevailing economic and market conditions, business cycle of
      業務週期，以及其他可能對本集團業           the Group’s business and other internal or external factors that
      務或財務表現及狀況產生影響的內部           may have an impact on the business or financial performance
      或外部因素；及                    and position of the Group; and

(f)   董事會可能認為相關及適當的任何其     (f)   any other factors that the Board may consider relevant and
      他因素。                       appropriate.

文化與價值觀                CULTURE AND VALUE
我們的競爭優勢植根於秉持誠信、問責與協   Our competitive strengths are underpinned by a disciplined and
作的嚴謹且績效驅動的文化。憑藉我們的泰   performance-driven culture grounded in integrity, accountability,
國傳統及產品開發能力、可擴展的輕資產業   and collaboration. Leveraging our Thai heritage and our product
務模式，以及多方面的營銷策略，本公司由   development capabilities, scalable asset-light business model,

本公司由具成效的董事會領導，董事會負責                    The Company is headed by an effective Board which assumes

司事務共同負責促進本公司取得成功。董事                    responsible for promoting the Company’s success by directing
客觀地作出決策，以符合本公司最佳利益。                    and supervising the Company’s affairs. Directors take decisions
                                       objectively in the best interests of the Company.

董事會具備適合本公司業務要求的技能、經                    The Board has a balance of skills, experience and diversity of
驗及多元觀點，並定期審核董事履行其對本                    perspectives appropriate to the requirements of the Company’s
公司職責所需作出的貢獻，以及董事是否有                    business and regularly reviews the contribution required from a
投入充足時間履行與其角色及董事會職責相                    Director to perform his/her responsibilities to the Company and
稱的職責。董事會由執行董事及非執行董事                    whether the Director is spending sufficient time performing them
（包括獨立非執行董事）組成，使董事會具有                   that are commensurate with their role and the Board responsibilities.
強大的獨立性，能夠有效行使獨立判斷。                     The Board includes a balanced composition of executive Directors
                                       and non-executive Directors (including independent non-executive
                                       Directors) so that there is a strong independent element on the

                      INVESTORS
本公司已採納股東通訊政策，當中包括股東   The Company has adopted the shareholders’ communication policy,
就影響本公司的各項事宜溝通意見的渠道，   which includes channels for shareholders to communicate their views
以及為徵求及了解股東及持份者意見而採取   on various matters affecting the Company, as well as steps taken to
的步驟。總括而言，股東通訊政策列明，本   solicit and understand the views of shareholders and stakeholders.
公司將透過年度報告及中期報告、公告、通   In summary, the shareholders’ communication policy states that the
函及股東大會與股東溝通，並將於本公司網   Company will communicate with shareholders through annual and
站維持資料。股東可透過電郵或電話向投資   interim reports, announcements, circulars and general meetings, and
者關係團隊提出查詢，本公司將及時作出回   will maintain information on the Company’s website. Shareholders
應。本公司亦鼓勵股東參與股東大會，並提   may submit enquiries to the Investor Relations team via email or
供機會讓股東提出問題及表達意見。      telephone, and the Company will respond in a timely manner. The
                      Company also encourages shareholder participation at general
                      meetings and provides opportunities for shareholders to raise
                      questions and express their views.

統。經考慮相關支持資料（包括從管理層獲       and internal control systems. After considering relevant supporting
得的確認；外聘外部及內部核數師開展的工       information, including confirmations from management; the work
作；及審核委員會的討論及推薦意見）後，       performed by the outsourced external and internal auditor; and

統就企業管治守則原則D2所載目的而言屬       Board has confirmed that the Company’s risk management and
適當及有效。                    internal control system are appropriate and effective for the purposes
                          set out in Principle D2 of the Corporate Governance Code.

董事會持續監督管理層設計、實施及監察風       The Board oversees management in the design, implementation and

風險管理與內部控制組織系統的特點          Characteristics of the Risk Management and Internal Control
                          Organisation System
本公司已建立結構化風險管理及內部控制框       The Company has established a structured risk management
架，以識別、評估及管理本集團面臨的重大       and internal control framework to identify, evaluate and manage
風險。                       significant risks faced by the Group.

該框架的主要特點包括：               The key features of the framework include:

•    風險識別及評估－管理層負責持續識     •    Risk Identification and Assessment – Management is
     別及評估主要業務、營運、財務、合          responsible for identifying and assessing key business,
     規及ESG相關風險。重大風險乃根據         operational, financial, compliance and ESG-related risks on an
     其可能性及潛在影響進行評估。            ongoing basis. Significant risks are evaluated based on their
                               likelihood and potential impact.

•    風險應對及控制活動－實施適當的政     •    Risk Response and Control Activities – Appropriate policies,
     策、程序及控制活動以減輕已識別的          procedures and control activities are implemented to mitigate
     風險。該等控制包括財務控制、營運          identified risks. These controls include financial controls,
     控制、合規控制及IT控制。             operational controls, compliance controls and IT controls.

•    監察及報告－管理層定期向審核委員     •    Monitoring and Reporting – Management provides regular
     會及董事會提供有關本公司重大風           updates to the Audit Committee and the Board regarding
     險、控制效果及補救進度的最新資料。         the Company’s significant risks, control effectiveness and
                               remediation progress.

況提供。                       how our audit addressed the matter is provided in that context.

批量折扣所產生的可變代價估計             Estimation of variable consideration arising from volume
                           rebates
貴集團於日常業務過程中向若干客戶提供批        The Group offers volume rebates to certain customers in the normal
量折扣。管理層於各報日期對各客戶的預期        course of business. Management makes an estimate at each
年度銷售額、基於過往客戶經驗的可變代價        reporting date on the annual sales expected for each customer, the
價值、預期客戶表現及╱或估計銷量進行估        value of the variable consideration based upon historical customer
計。該等估計批量折扣確認為收入減項，且        experience, expected customer performance and/or estimated
不被視為與向客戶銷售產品相關的獨立履約        sales volumes. The estimated volume rebates are recognised
義務。                        as a reduction from revenue and are not considered a separate
                           performance obligation from the sale of products to the customers.

於2025年12月31日，批量折扣的估計金額     As at 31 December 2025, the estimated volume rebates amounting
為2,022,000美元，已計入其他應付款項，    to US$2,022,000 have been recorded in Other Payables, as
詳情載於財務報表附註24。吾等將批量折        disclosed in Note 24 to the financial statements. We identified
扣所產生的可變代價估計識別為關鍵審計事        estimation of variable consideration arising from volume rebates
項，原因是該釐定涉及管理層的重大判斷及        as a key audit matter as the determination involves significant
估計不確定性。                    management’s judgement and estimation uncertainty.

關鍵審計事項（續）                            KEY AUDIT MATTERS (continued)
批量折扣所產生的可變代價估計（續）                    Estimation of variable consideration arising from volume
                                     rebates (continued)
吾等評估批量折扣會計處理的審計程序包                   Our audit procedures to assess accounting for volume rebates
括：                                   included:

•   了解與批量折扣所產生的可變代價估                 •    Obtaining an understanding of management’s processes and
    計相關的管理層流程及監控措施；                       controls relating to the estimation of variable consideration
                                          arising from volume rebates;

•   審閱客戶合約，以確定與批量折扣相                 •    Reviewing customer contracts to identify terms and
    關的條款及條件，以及識別享有批量                      conditions relating to the volume rebates and completeness of
    折扣資格的客戶的完整性；                          identification of customers entitled to volume rebates;

•   通過比較歷史數據與 貴集團已記錄                 •    Assessing reasonableness of the estimated variable
    的實際批量折扣，評估批量折扣所產                      consideration arising from volume rebates by comparing

款 項 19,235,000 美 元 ， 佔 貴 集 團 資 產 總   amounting to US$19,235,000, which represented 10% of total assets

損」）撥備62,000美元，詳見財務報表附註               US$62,000, as disclosed in Note 19 to the financial statements.
19。貿易應收款項預期信貸虧損撥備是根據                 Allowance for ECL for trade receivables is assessed based on the

信貸虧損模型評估。

貴集團通過對信貸減值的債務人進行個別評                  The Group determines allowance for ECL of trade receivables by
估，並採用預期信貸虧損撥備矩陣法估計其                  making debtor-specific assessment for credit-impaired debtors
餘債務人的存續期預期信貸虧損，以釐定貿                  and using a provision matrix method to estimate lifetime ECL for
易應收款項預期信貸虧損撥備。準備率乃根                  the remaining debtors. The allowance rates are based on days
據逾期天數分組計算。該撥備矩陣最初乃根                  past due groupings. The allowance matrix is initially based on the
據 貴集團過往觀察違約率確定。 貴集團                  Group’s historical observed default rates. The Group will calibrate
將校準該撥備矩陣，以納入前瞻性資料調整                  the provision matrix to adjust historical credit loss experience with
過往信貸虧損經驗。於各報告日期， 貴集                  forward-looking information. At every reporting date, the Group
團均會更新過往違約率，並分析前瞻性估計                  updates historical default rates and analyses changes in the forward-
的變動。                                 looking estimates.

             際）」）編製。

             就新加坡財務報告準則（國際）而                        For the purpose of SFRS(I), financial statements that have

     2.6   附屬公司                   2.6   Subsidiary
           附屬公司為本集團所控制的被投               A subsidiary is an investee that is controlled by the
           資對象。當本集團因參與被投資               Group. The Group controls an investee when it is
           對象的營運而獲得或有權享有其               exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
           可變回報，並能夠運用其對被投               involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect
           資對象的權力影響上述回報，本               those returns through its power over the investee.
           集團即對該實體擁有控制權。

           賬。                           impairment losses.

      2.7    廠房及設備                                2.7   Plant and equipment
             所有廠房及設備項目初步按成本                             All items of plant and equipment are initially recorded at
             入賬。於確認後，廠房及設備                              cost. Subsequent to recognition, plant and equipment
             按成本減累計折舊及任何累計減                             are measured at cost less accumulated depreciation
             值虧損（如有）計量。廠房及設備                            and any accumulated impairment losses, if any. The cost
             的成本包括其購買價格以及使資                             of plant and equipment includes its purchase price and
             產能夠按照管理層預期的方式運                             any costs directly attributable to bringing the asset to
             作而直接導致的任何成本。若因                             the location and condition necessary for it to be capable
             取得或使用廠房及設備而產生拆                             of operating in the manner intended by management.
             除、搬遷或恢復的義務，則拆                              Dismantlement, removal or restoration costs are
             除、搬遷或恢復的成本應作為廠                             included as part of the cost of plant and equipment if
             房及設備成本的一部分。                                the obligation for dismantlement, removal or restoration
                                                        is incurred as a consequence of acquiring or using the
                                                        plant and equipment

             折舊乃於以下資產估計可使用年                             Depreciation is computed on a straight-line basis over
             期按直線法計算：                                   the estimated useful lives of the assets as follows:

             電腦                       3至5年              Computers                                       3–5 years
             辦公室設備                    3至5年              Office equipment                                3–5 years
             傢俬及裝置                      5年              Furniture and fittings                            5 years
             模具                         5年              Mould                                             5 years

             在建工程不予折舊，因為該等資                             Construction in progress are not depreciated as these
             產尚未可供使用。                                   assets are not yet available for use.

      2.10 非金融資產減值                          2.10 Impairment of non-financial assets
           本集團於各報告日期評估是否有                        The Group assesses at each reporting date whether
           跡象顯示資產可能出現減值。倘                        there is an indication that an asset may be impaired.
           有任何跡象時，或當需要對資產                        If any indication exists, or when an annual impairment
           進行年度減值測試時，本集團將                        testing for an asset is required, the Group makes an
           估計該資產的可收回金額。                          estimate of the asset’s recoverable amount.

             資產的可收回金額為資產或現金                       An asset’s recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s
             產生單位的公平值扣減出售成本                       or cash-generating unit’s fair value less costs of disposal
             與其使用價值兩者中的較高者，                       and its value in use and is determined for an individual
             並就個別資產而釐定，除非該資                       asset, unless the asset does not generate cash inflows
             產不能產生基本上獨立於其他資                       that are largely independent of those from other assets
             產或資產組合所產生的現金流                        or group of assets. Where the carrying amount of an asset
             入。當資產或現金產生單位的賬                       or cash-generating unit exceeds its recoverable amount,
             面值超過其可回收金額時，該資                       the asset is considered impaired and is written down to
             產被視為已減值並撇減至其可回                       its recoverable amount.
             收金額。

             減值虧損於損益確認。                           Impairment losses are recognised in profit or loss.

             過往確認的減值虧損僅在上次確                       A previously recognised impairment loss is reversed only
             認減值虧損以來，用以釐定資產                       if there has been a change in the assumptions used to
             可收回金額的假設出現變動時，                       determine the asset’s recoverable amount since the last
             方可撥回。撥回數額以資產賬面                       impairment loss was recognised. The reversal is limited
             值不超過其可收回金額，亦不超                       so that the carrying amount of the asset does not exceed
             過倘過往年度並無就資產確認減                       its recoverable amount, nor exceed the carrying amount
             值虧損而釐定的賬面值（扣除折                       that would have been determined, net of depreciation,
             舊後）為限。該撥回數額於損益                       had no impairment loss been recognised for the asset
             確認。                                  in prior years. Such reversal is recognised in the profit or
                                                  loss.

                                 INFORMATION (continued)
     2.12 金融資產減值                 2.12 Impairment of financial assets
          本集團就所有並非以按公平值計              The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit
          入損益的方式持有的債務工具及              losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair
          財務擔保合約確認預期信貸虧損              value through profit or loss and financial guarantee
         （「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信             contracts. ECLs are based on the difference between
          貸虧損基於根據合約到期的合約              the contractual cash flows due in accordance with the
          現金流量與本集團預期收取的所              contract and all the cash flows that the Group expects
          有現金流量之間的差額而計算，              to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original
          並按原實際利率的近似值貼現。              effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will
          預期現金流量將包括來自出售所              include cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other
          持抵押品的現金流量或組成合約              credit enhancements that are integral to the contractual
          條款不可分割部分的其他信貸提              terms.
          升措施。

        預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。                 ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures
        就初步確認以來信貸風險並無大                 for which there has not been a significant increase in
        幅增加的信貸敞口而言，會為未                 credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided
        來12個月可能發生的違約事件                 for credit losses that results from default events that are
        所產生的信貸虧損（12個月預期                possible within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL).
        信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損撥                 For those credit exposures for which there has been a
        備。就初始確認以來信貸風險大                 significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,
        幅增加的信貸敞口而言，須就預                 a loss allowance is recognised for credit losses expected
        期於敞口的餘下年期產生的信貸                 over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of
        虧損計提減值撥備（無論違約時                 timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).
        間如何）（全期預期信貸虧損）
                     。

        就貿易應收款項而言，本集團應                 For trade receivables, the Group applies a simplified
        用簡化方法計算預期信貸虧損。                 approach in calculating ECLs. Therefore, the Group does
        因此，本集團並無追蹤信貸風險                 not track changes in credit risk, but instead recognises a

        全期預期信貸虧損確認虧損撥                  date. The Group has established a provision matrix that is

        損經驗建立撥備矩陣，並就債務                 forward-looking factors specific to the debtors and the

        作出調整。

        倘合約付款逾期90日，本集團會                The Group considers a financial asset in default when
        視該項金融資產違約。然而，在                 contractual payments are 90 days past due. However,

      2.13 存貨                              2.13 Inventories
           存貨乃按成本及可變現淨值兩者                       Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net
           之間的較低者入賬。成本按先入                       realisable value. Cost is calculated using the first-in
           先出法計算，並且包括所有採購                       first-out method and comprises all costs of purchase,
           成本、轉換成本及將存貨運送至                       costs of conversion and other costs incurred in bringing
           現址及達致現狀所產生的其他成                       the inventories to their present location and condition.
           本。可變現淨值為於日常業務過                       Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in
           程中的估計售價，扣減進行銷售                       the ordinary course of business, less estimated costs
           所需的估計成本。                             necessary to make the sale.

             倘有需要，為損壞、過期及滯銷                     Where necessary, allowance is provided for damaged,
             項目提供撥備，以將存貨的賬面                     expired and slow-moving items to adjust the carrying
             值調整至成本與可變現淨值兩者                     value of inventories to the lower of cost and net realisable
             中的較低者。                             value.

      2.14 現金及現金等價物                        2.14 Cash and cash equivalents
           現金及現金等價物包括銀行現                        Cash and cash equivalents comprise cash at bank which
           金，其價值變動風險並不重大。                       are subject to an insignificant risk of changes in value.
           當中亦包括構成本集團現金管理                       These also include bank overdrafts that form an integral
           不可分割部分的銀行透支。                         part of the Group’s cash management.

      2.15 撥備                              2.15 Provisions
           當本集團因過去的事件而須承擔                       Provisions are recognised when the Group has a
           現有責任（法定或推定），且因清                      present obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of a
           償有關責任很可能需要流出包含                       past event, it is probable that an outflow of resources
           經濟利益在內的資源，而有關責                       embodying economic benefits will be required to settle
           任涉及金額能可靠地估計時，則                       the obligation and the amount of the obligation can be
           會確認撥備。                               estimated reliably.

             撥備於各報告期末進行檢討，並                     Provisions are reviewed at the end of each reporting
             作出調整以反映現時最佳估計。                     period and adjusted to reflect the current best estimate.
             倘不再可能需要流出經濟資源以                     If it is no longer probable that an outflow of economic
             清償責任，則撥備予以撥回。倘                     resources will be required to settle the obligation, the
             貨幣時間價值的影響重大，則使                     provision is reversed. If the effect of the time value of
             用反映（如適用）責任特定風險的                    money is material, provisions are discounted using a
             當前稅前利率貼現撥備。當使用                     current pre-tax rate that reflects, where appropriate, the
             貼現法，隨著時間過去而增加的                     risks specific to the liability. When discounting is used,
             撥備確認為融資成本。                         the increase in the provision due to the passage of time is
                                                recognised as a finance cost.

     編製本集團的財務報表時，管理層須            The preparation of the Group’s financial statements requires
     作出會影響於各報告期末時收益、開            management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions
     支、資產及負債的呈報金額以及或有            that affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses,
     負債披露的判斷、估計及假設。有關            assets and liabilities, and the disclosure of contingent liabilities
     該等假設及估計的不確定性可能導致            at the end of each reporting period. Uncertainty about these
     需要對未來期間受影響的資產或負債            assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes that
     的賬面值作出重大調整。                 require a material adjustment to the carrying amount of the
                                 asset or liability affected in the future periods.

25. 使用權資產及租賃負債                25. R I G H T - O F - U S E A S S E T S A N D L E A S E
                                  LIABILITIES
  本集團擁有用於其業務營運的辦公室                The Group has lease contracts for offices and motor vehicles
  及汽車的租賃合約。本集團於其租賃                used in its operations. The Group’s obligation under its leases is
  項下的責任由出租人對租賃資產的所                secured by the lessor’s title to the leased assets. Generally, the
  有權作抵押。一般而言，本集團被限                Group is restricted from assigning and subleasing the leased
  制轉讓及轉租租賃資產。                     assets.

   本集團就若干租賃應用「短期租賃」及              The Group applies the ‘short-term lease’ and ‘lease of low-
  「低價值資產租賃」的確認豁免。                 value assets’ recognition exemptions for certain leases.

  換算儲備：換算儲備包括因換算功能                        Translation reserve: The translation reserve comprises foreign
  貨幣與本集團呈列貨幣不同的附屬公                        exchange differences arising from the translation of the financial
  司財務報表而產生的匯兌差額。                          statements of the subsidiary corporations whose functional
                                          currencies are different from that of the Group’s presentation
                                          currency.

  合併儲備：合併儲備指涉及共同控制                        Merger reserve: The merger reserve represents acquisition
  實體的收購。該儲備源於在共同控制                        involving entities under common control. The reserve arises
  下收購的附屬公司之間的購買代價與                        from the difference between the purchase considerations and
  股本差額。                                   the share capital of the subsidiaries acquired under common
                                          control.

  界定福利責任儲備：本集團對泰國員                        Defined benefit obligation reserve: The Group has defined
  工有界定福利責任，而界定福利責任                        benefit obligations to employees in Thailand and the defined
  儲備包括因離職後福利產生的精算收                        benefit obligation reserve comprises of actuarial gains and
  益及虧損，並即時於股東權益中確認。                       losses arising from post-employment benefits that are

33. 財務風險管理目標及政策           33. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES
                              AND POLICIES
  本集團因其業務營運及使用金融工具           The Group is exposed to financial risks arising from its
  而面臨財務風險。主要財務風險包括           operations and the use of financial instruments. The key
  信貸風險、外幣風險及流動資金風            financial risks include credit risk, foreign currency risk and
  險。董事會審閱及協定管理該等風險           liquidity risk. The board of directors reviews and agrees policies
  的政策及程序。                    and procedures for the management of these risks.

  以下各節提供有關本集團所承擔上述           The following sections provide details regarding the Group’s
  財務風險的詳情及管理該等風險的目           exposure to the above-mentioned financial risks and the
  標、政策及程序。                   objectives, policies and processes for the management of
                             these risks.

  (a)   信貸風險                 (a)   Credit risk
        信貸風險是指交易對手將無法履             Credit risk is the risk that a counterparty will not meet
        行其於金融工具或客戶合約項下             its obligations under a financial instrument or customer
        之責任而引致財務損失的風險。             contract, leading to a financial loss. The Group’s
        本集團面臨的信貸風險主要來自             exposure to credit risk arises primarily from trade and
        貿易及其他應收款項。就其他金             other receivables. For other financial assets (including
        融資產（包括銀行現金）而言，本            cash at banks), the Group minimises credit risk by
        集團透過僅與高信貸評級的對手             dealing exclusively with high credit rating counterparties.
        方進行交易以將信貸風險減至最
        低。

        本集團的目標是在尋求收益持續             The Group’s objective is to seek continual revenue
        增長的同時，將因信貸風險增加             growth while minimising losses incurred due to
        而產生的虧損降至最低。本集團             increased credit risk exposure. The Group trades only
        僅與獲認可及信譽良好的第三方             with recognised and creditworthy third parties. It is the
        進行交易。本集團的政策是，所             Group’s policy that all customers who wish to trade on
        有希望按信用條款交易的客戶須             credit terms are subject to credit verification procedures.
        進行信用驗證程序。此外，應收             In addition, receivable balances are monitored on an
        款項結餘乃受到持續監察，故本             ongoing basis with the result that the Group’s exposure
        集團的壞賬風險並不重大。所有             to bad debts is not significant. All credit terms and
        信貸條款及信貸額度均須根據本             credit limits are subject to approval in accordance
        集團的政策獲得批准。抵押品安             with the Group’s policy. Measures such as collateral
        排及貿易應收款項保理等措施均             arrangements and factoring of trade receivables are used
        用作減低信貸風險。                  to mitigate credit risk.

        本集團在資產初始確認時考慮違             The Group considers the probability of default upon
        約的可能性，並評估於各報告期             initial recognition of asset and whether there has been
        信貸風險是否持續顯著增加。              a significant increase in credit risk on an ongoing basis
                                   throughout each reporting period.

        當多個交易對手從事類似的商業                      Concentrations arise when a number of counterparties
        活動，或在同一地區從事類似                       are engaged in similar business activities, or activities
        的活動，或其經濟特點會導致其                      in the same geographical region, or have economic
        履行合約責任的能力受到經濟、                      features that would cause their ability to meet contractual
        政治或其他狀況變化的類似影響                      obligations to be similarly affected by changes in
        時，便會出現集中情況。集中程                      economic, political or other conditions. Concentrations
        度表明本集團表現對影響特定行                      indicate the relative sensitivity of the Group’s
        業發展的相對敏感度（請參閱下                      performance to developments affecting a particular

  (c)   流動資金風險                       (c)   Liquidity risk
        流動資金風險乃本集團因資金短                     Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter
        缺而難以履行財務責任的風險。                     difficulty in meeting financial obligations due to shortage
        本集團面對的流動資金風險主要                     of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity risk arises
        來自金融資產及負債到期日錯                      primarily from mismatches of the maturities of financial
        配。                                 assets and liabilities.

        本集團的流動資金風險管理政策                     The Group’s liquidity risk management policy is to
        是以集團為基礎管理流動資金                      manage liquidity risk on a group basis, to maintain
        風險，以維持充足的流動金融資                     sufficient liquid financial assets. The Group finances its
        產。本集團通過營運產生之資金                     working capital requirements through funds generated
        以撥付營運資金需求。                         from operations.

             本集團資本管理的主要目標乃確                    The primary objective of the Group’s capital management
             保維持強勁的信貸評級及穩健的                    is to ensure that it maintains a strong credit rating and
             資本比率，以支持其業務及將股                    healthy capital ratios in order to support its business and
             東價值擴至最大。本集團之資本                    maximise shareholder value. The capital structure of the
             架構包括本公司擁有人應佔權益                    Group consists of equity attributable to owners of the
            （包括股本及儲備）。                         Company, comprising share capital and reserves.

             本集團管理其資本架構，並根據                    The Group manages its capital structure and makes
             經濟情況變動對其作出調整。                     adjustment to it in light of changes in economic
             為管理資本結構，本集團可調整                    conditions. To manage the capital structure, the Group
             向股東派付的股息、向股東返還                    may adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return
             資本、回購股份或發行新股。年                    capital to shareholders, repurchase shares or issue new
             內，本集團已宣派股息（誠如附                    shares. For the year, the Group has declared dividends as
             註14所披露）。                          disclosed in Note 14.

             於年內，目標、政策及程序概無                    No changes were made in the objectives, policies and
             作出變動。                             processes during the year.

             由於本集團於各報告期間期末處                    As the Group is at net cash position at the end of the

             比率並無意義。                           meaningful.

34. 資產及負債的公平值            34. FAIR VALUE OF ASSETS AND LIABILITIES
  公平值層級                     Fair value hierarchy
  本集團將公平值計量分類為以下層           The Group categorises fair value measurements using a fair
  級，該層級取決於所使用的估值輸入：         value hierarchy that is dependent on the valuation inputs used
                            as follows:

  •   第一級－本集團於計量日期可取        •     Level 1 – Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for
      得的相同資產或負債於活躍市場              identical assets or liabilities that the Group can access at
      的報價（未經調整），                  the measurement date,

  •   第二級－其他直接或間接可觀察        •     Level 2 – Inputs other than quoted prices included within
      資產或負債的輸入數據（第一級              Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liability, either
      包括之報價除外），及                  directly or indirectly, and

  •   第三級－資產或負債的不可觀察        •     Level 3 – Unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.
      輸入數據。

  由於最低層級的輸入數據對整體計量          Fair value measurements that use inputs of different hierarchy
  而言最為重要，因此用來計量公平值          levels are categorised in its entirety in the same level of the fair
  所使用的輸入數據將被作為一個整體          value hierarchy as the lowest level input that is significant to the
  而分類至公平值層級的同一層級。           entire measurement.

  第二級公平值計量 — 遠期貨幣合約一        Level 2 fair value measurements – forward currency contracts
  般採用市場可觀察輸入數據（如遠期定         are generally valued using a valuation technique with market
  價模型）的估值法進行估值，包括交易         observable inputs such as forward pricing models which
  對手的信貸質素、外匯即期及遠期匯          incorporate inputs including the credit quality of counterparties,
  率以及遠期匯率曲線等輸入數據。於          foreign exchange spot and forward rates and forward rate
  2025年12月31日，遠期貨幣合約的賬      curves. As at 31 December 2025, the carrying amount of
  面值與其公平值相若。                forward currencycontracts approximates its fair value.

  並非按公平值列賬且其賬面值與公平          Financial assets and liabilities not carried at fair
  值合理相若之金融資產及金融負債           value and whose carrying amounts are reasonable
                            approximation of fair value
  管理層已釐定銀行現金、即期貿易及          Management has determined that the carrying amount of cash
  其他應收款項、即期貿易及其他應付          at banks, current trade and other receivables, current trade
  款項、應計經營開支及應收貸款的賬          and other payables, accrued operating expenses and loan
  面值與其公平值合理相若，原因為該          receivables, reasonably approximate their fair values because
  等款項大部分屬短期性質或按接近市          these are mostly short term in nature or subject to interest rate
  場利率的利率計息。                 close to market rate of interest.
